LLMs operate strictly as diagnostic instruments that assess population state and generate structured risk evaluations, while deterministic formulas with explicit bounds translate these assessments into traceable parameter updates. This separation ensures that every policy decision can be attributed to inspectable rules while maintaining adaptive response to emergent needs. We validate the framework through systematic ablation across five experimental conditions in elderly care simulation. Results demonstrate that explicit control rules outperform end-to-end black-box LLM approaches by 11.7\% while preserving full auditability, confirming that transparency need not compromise adaptive performance.

</details>


### [175] [Ran Score: a LLM-based Evaluation Score for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22935)
*Ran Zhang,Yucong Lin,Zhaoli Su,Bowen Liu,Danni Ai,Tianyu Fu,Deqiang Xiao,Jingfan Fan,Yuanyuan Wang,Mingwei Gao,Yuwan Hu,Shuya Gao,Jingtao Li,Jian Yang,Hong Song,Hongliang Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个临床医生指导的框架，结合大型语言模型从胸部X光报告中提取多标签发现，并定义了Ran Score评估指标。实验表明该框架能显著提升对低流行率异常的识别能力，宏平均分数提升至0.956，优于CheXbert基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X光报告生成和自动评估受到以下因素限制：对低流行率异常的识别能力差，以及对临床重要语言（包括否定和歧义）的处理不足。

Method: 开发了一个临床医生指导的框架，结合人类专业知识和大型语言模型，用于从自由文本胸部X光报告中提取多标签发现，并使用该框架定义Ran Score（发现级别的报告评估指标）。使用来自公共胸部X光数据集的三个非重叠MIMIC-CXR-EN队列和一个独立的ChestX-CN验证队列，优化提示、建立放射科医生衍生的参考标签并评估报告生成模型。

Result: 优化后的框架在MIMIC-CXR-EN开发队列上使宏平均分数从0.753提高到0.956，在直接可比较标签上比CheXbert基准高出15.7个百分点，并在ChestX-CN验证队列上显示出稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Clinician-guided prompt optimization能够提高与放射科医生制定参考标准的一致性，Ran Score能够在发现级别对报告保真度进行评估，特别是对于低流行率异常情况。

Abstract: Chest X-ray report generation and automated evaluation are limited by poor recognition of low-prevalence abnormalities and inadequate handling of clinically important language, including negation and ambiguity. We develop a clinician-guided framework combining human expertise and large language models for multi-label finding extraction from free-text chest X-ray reports and use it to define Ran Score, a finding-level metric for report evaluation. Using three non-overlapping MIMIC-CXR-EN cohorts from a public chest X-ray dataset and an independent ChestX-CN validation cohort, we optimize prompts, establish radiologist-derived reference labels and evaluate report generation models. The optimized framework improves the macro-averaged score from 0.753 to 0.956 on the MIMIC-CXR-EN development cohort, exceeds the CheXbert benchmark by 15.7 percentage points on directly comparable labels, and shows robust generalization on the ChestX-CN validation cohort. Here we show that clinician-guided prompt optimization improves agreement with a radiologist-derived reference standard and that Ran Score enables finding-level evaluation of report fidelity, particularly for low-prevalence abnormalities.

</details>


### [176] [Optimizing Small Language Models for NL2SQL via Chain-of-Thought Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22942)
*Anshul Solanki,Sanchit Latawa,Koushik Chakraborty,Navneet Kamboj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究发现在NL2SQL任务中存在反直觉的缩放现象：大模型微调收益极低，而小模型通过微调和CoT推理增强可实现从36%到54.5%的显著提升，虽不及大模型精度，但有效降低了部署成本和延迟，满足生产环境需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型在高推理成本与规模化部署之间的矛盾，寻求一种既能满足业务性能要求又具有成本效益的NL2SQL解决方案。

Method: 在大模型（Gemini 2.5 Flash/Lite）和小模型（Qwen）上进行NL2SQL任务的微调实验，并进一步在小模型上引入显式链式思考（CoT）推理进行数据增强。

Result: 大模型微调收益甚微且易过拟合；小模型基线从36%提升至45%，引入CoT推理后进一步提升至54.5%，虽低于Gemini 2.5等大模型，但已满足业务关键性能阈值。

Conclusion: 小模型（Qwen）通过微调和链式思考（CoT）推理模式增强，可在降低计算成本和延迟的同时达到生产级性能阈值，为NL2SQL任务提供了一种计算高效且经济可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Translating Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) remains a critical bottleneck for democratization of data in enterprises. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) like Gemini 2.5 and other LLMs have demonstrated impressive zero-shot capabilities, their high inference costs limit deployment at scale. This paper explores the efficacy of fine-tuning both large and small language models on NL2SQL tasks.
  Our research reveals a counter-intuitive scaling phenomenon. Fine-tuning large models (Gemini 2.5 Flash/Lite) on standard datasets yields negligible returns, often leading to overfitting on complex queries. Conversely, small models (Qwen) show significant gains. Fine-tuning improved the small model baseline from 36% to 45%, and further enriching the dataset with explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning surged accuracy to 54.5%(Fig 2). While this is still lower than the accuracy of large models like Gemini 2.5 , it does serve the business goal of significant cost reduction, latency in inference time and also meeting the business critical performance accuracy threshold.This paper demonstrates that transferring reasoning patterns enables compute-efficient smaller models to approach production-grade performance.

</details>


### [177] [PersonalQ: Select, Quantize, and Serve Personalized Diffusion Models for Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22943)
*Qirui Wang,Qi Guo,Yiding Sun,Junkai Yang,Dongxu Zhang,Shanmin Pang,Qing Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PersonalQ是一个统一的个性化文本到图像生成服务框架，通过共享的trigger token机制连接checkpoint选择和量化。Check-in组件利用意图感知混合检索和LLM重排序进行准确的checkpoint选择；TAQ组件在交叉注意力层保留trigger条件的key/value表示，同时对其他路径进行激进量化，在保证个性化概念完整性的同时实现高效推理服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化文本到图像生成面临两大服务难题：1) 自然语言请求存在歧义，易被误路由到视觉相似的checkpoint；2) 标准后训练量化会扭曲编码个性化概念的脆弱表示。

Method: 提出PersonalQ统一框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Check-in：结合意图感知的混合检索与LLM重排序进行checkpoint选择，仅在多意图模糊时请求用户确认，并插入所选checkpoint的规范trigger token进行提示重写；2) Trigger-Aware Quantization (TAQ)：在交叉注意力层应用trigger感知的混合精度，保留trigger条件下的key/value行及其注意力权重，同时对其他路径进行激进量化以节省内存。

Result: 实验表明，PersonalQ在意图对齐方面优于检索和重排序基线方法；TAQ相比现有扩散模型PTQ方法提供更优的压缩-质量权衡，使得大规模服务个性化checkpoint而不牺牲保真度成为可能。

Conclusion: PersonalQ通过统一的trigger token机制有效解决了个性化图像生成的两大挑战：Check-in组件通过意图感知的混合检索和LLM重排序显著提升了意图对齐效果，TAQ组件则在交叉注意力层应用trigger感知的混合精度量化，在保证个性化概念表示完整性的同时实现了高效推理。该框架为大规模服务个性化检查点提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Personalized text-to-image generation lets users fine-tune diffusion models into repositories of concept-specific checkpoints, but serving these repositories efficiently is difficult for two reasons: natural-language requests are often ambiguous and can be misrouted to visually similar checkpoints, and standard post-training quantization can distort the fragile representations that encode personalized concepts. We present PersonalQ, a unified framework that connects checkpoint selection and quantization through a shared signal -- the checkpoint's trigger token. Check-in performs intent-aligned selection by combining intent-aware hybrid retrieval with LLM-based reranking over checkpoint context and asks a brief clarification question only when multiple intents remain plausible; it then rewrites the prompt by inserting the selected checkpoint's canonical trigger. Complementing this, Trigger-Aware Quantization (TAQ) applies trigger-aware mixed precision in cross-attention, preserving trigger-conditioned key/value rows (and their attention weights) while aggressively quantizing the remaining pathways for memory-efficient inference. Experiments show that PersonalQ improves intent alignment over retrieval and reranking baselines, while TAQ consistently offers a stronger compression-quality trade-off than prior diffusion PTQ methods, enabling scalable serving of personalized checkpoints without sacrificing fidelity.

</details>


### [178] [Where Experts Disagree, Models Fail: Detecting Implicit Legal Citations in French Court Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22973)
*Avrile Floro,Tamara Dhorasoo,Soline Pellez,Nils Holzenberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过1,015个法国民法典条款注释对数据集，探讨法院在判决中隐含引用法规的频率。监督模型达到F1=0.70，但68%的误报来自注释者存在分歧的33%案例，表明模型失败可预测注释者分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To investigate how often courts implicitly apply statutory rules, addressing the challenge of distinguishing legal reasoning from semantic similarity in computational legal analysis.

Method: Created a benchmark of 1,015 passage-article pairs annotated by three legal experts, and built a supervised ensemble model for detecting implicit citations to the French Civil Code in first-instance court decisions.

Result: The supervised ensemble achieves F1 = 0.70 (77% accuracy). An unsupervised approach using top-k ranking and multi-model consensus yields 76% precision at k = 200. Key finding: 68% of false positives fall on the 33% of cases where annotators disagreed.

Conclusion: Despite moderate inter-annotator agreement (κ = 0.33), the supervised ensemble achieves F1 = 0.70. The asymmetry in errors (68% of false positives on 33% disagreement cases) reveals that model failures correlate with expert disagreement. An unsupervised approach using top-k ranking and multi-model consensus achieves 76% precision at k = 200. False positives tend to represent legally ambiguous applications rather than obvious errors, suggesting computational legal analysis can surface meaningful borderline cases.

Abstract: Computational methods applied to legal scholarship hold the promise of analyzing law at scale. We start from a simple question: how often do courts implicitly apply statutory rules? This requires distinguishing legal reasoning from semantic similarity. We focus on implicit citation of the French Civil Code in first-instance court decisions and introduce a benchmark of 1,015 passage-article pairs annotated by three legal experts. We show that expert disagreement predicts model failures. Inter-annotator agreement is moderate ($κ$ = 0.33) with 43% of disagreements involving the boundary between factual description and legal reasoning. Our supervised ensemble achieves F1 = 0.70 (77% accuracy), but this figure conceals an asymmetry: 68% of false positives fall on the 33% of cases where the annotators disagreed. Despite these limits, reframing the task as top-k ranking and leveraging multi-model consensus yields 76% precision at k = 200 in an unsupervised setting. Moreover, the remaining false positives tend to surface legally ambiguous applications rather than obvious errors.

</details>


### [179] [JFTA-Bench: Evaluate LLM's Ability of Tracking and Analyzing Malfunctions Using Fault Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22978)
*Yuhui Wang,Zhixiong Yang,Ming Zhang,Shihan Dou,Zhiheng Xi,Enyu Zhou,Senjie Jin,Yujiong Shen,Dingwei Zhu,Yi Dong,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出故障树的新型文本表示方法，构建了包含3130条条目、平均40.75轮的多轮对话基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在复杂环境中协助故障定位的能力，并引入模糊信息生成和回滚恢复机制模拟用户错误场景，Gemini 2.5 Pro表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故障树常以图像形式存储，无法被大语言模型直接处理，难以用于追踪和分析故障的多轮对话辅助任务。需要将故障树转换为文本表示形式，并构建评估基准来测试模型在复杂环境中的交互能力和错误恢复能力。

Method: 1) 提出故障树的新型文本表示方法，使图像形式的故障树能被大语言模型直接处理；2) 构建包含3130条条目、平均每条目40.75轮的多轮对话基准测试；3) 训练端到端模型生成模糊信息以反映用户行为；4) 引入长距离回滚和恢复程序模拟用户错误场景。

Result: 该基准测试包含3130个条目，平均每条目40.75轮对话。Gemini 2.5 Pro在所有模型中取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: Gemini 2.5 Pro在多轮对话系统的复杂环境交互评估中取得最佳性能，验证了所提出方法的有效性。该基准测试和模型的构建为故障树分析的多轮对话辅助提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: In the maintenance of complex systems, fault trees are used to locate problems and provide targeted solutions. To enable fault trees stored as images to be directly processed by large language models, which can assist in tracking and analyzing malfunctions, we propose a novel textual representation of fault trees. Building on it, we construct a benchmark for multi-turn dialogue systems that emphasizes robust interaction in complex environments, evaluating a model's ability to assist in malfunction localization, which contains $3130$ entries and $40.75$ turns per entry on average. We train an end-to-end model to generate vague information to reflect user behavior and introduce long-range rollback and recovery procedures to simulate user error scenarios, enabling assessment of a model's integrated capabilities in task tracking and error recovery, and Gemini 2.5 pro archives the best performance.

</details>


### [180] [On the use of Aggregation Operators to improve Human Identification using Dental Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23003)
*Antonio D. Villegas-Yeguas,Guillermo R-García,Tzipi Kahana,Jorge Pinares Toledo,Esi Sharon,Oscar Ibañez,Oscar Cordón*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出三种聚合机制改进法医牙科牙科记录自动比对，白盒机器学习技术将平均排名从3.91提升至2.02-2.21，兼具高性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前法医牙科中自动牙科记录比对方法存在局限性：要么使用简单技术未能充分利用比较信息，要么因缺乏同行评审出版物而导致内部行为不透明。需要设计可被专家理解和验证的聚合机制来改进现有方法。

Method: 提出了三种聚合方法：1）数据驱动的词典排序聚合；2）模糊逻辑聚合方法；3）机器学习技术作为聚合机制。基于七种不同标准的现成编码方法进行对比验证。

Result: 使用来自两个不同人群的215个法医案例进行验证。结果表明白盒机器学习技术（平均排名2.02-2.21）优于现有先进方法（平均排名3.91），同时不影响方法的可解释性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 白盒机器学习技术作为聚合模型能够在保持方法可解释性和可验证性的同时，将平均排名从3.91（当前先进水平）提升至2.02-2.21，显著改善法医牙科中牙科记录比对的性能。

Abstract: The comparison of dental records is a standardized technique in forensic dentistry used to speed up the identification of individuals in multiple-comparison scenarios. Specifically, the odontogram comparison is a procedure to compute criteria that will be used to perform a ranking. State-of-the-art automatic methods either make use of simple techniques, without utilizing the full potential of the information obtained from a comparison, or their internal behavior is not known due to the lack of peer-reviewed publications. This work aims to design aggregation mechanisms to automatically compare pairs of dental records that can be understood and validated by experts, improving the current methods. To do so, we introduce different aggregation approaches using the state-of-the-art codification, based on seven different criteria. In particular, we study the performance of i) data-driven lexicographical order-based aggregations, ii) well-known fuzzy logic aggregation methods and iii) machine learning techniques as aggregation mechanisms. To validate our proposals, 215 forensic cases from two different populations have been used. The results obtained show how the use of white-box machine learning techniques as aggregation models (average ranking from 2.02 to 2.21) are able to improve the state-of-the-art (average ranking of 3.91) without compromising the explainability and interpretability of the method.

</details>


### [181] [Can Large Language Models Reason and Optimize Under Constraints?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23004)
*Fabien Bernier,Salah Ghamizi,Pantelis Dogoulis,Maxime Cordy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大型语言模型在最优功率流(OPF)问题约束下的推理和优化能力。研究发现，即使是当前最先进的大型语言模型和推理型LLM，在处理涉及物理和操作约束的结构化优化问题时也普遍失败，揭示了LLM在约束推理方面的关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然在各种自然语言任务中展现出强大能力，但其解决具有约束条件的抽象和优化问题的能力仍缺乏探索。该研究旨在探究LLM是否能够在最优功率流(OPF)问题的物理和操作约束下进行推理和优化。

Method: 构建了一个具有挑战性的评估框架，该框架需要多种基础技能，包括推理、结构化输入处理、算术运算和约束优化。评估针对最优功率流(OPF)问题的物理和操作约束进行测试。

Result: 评估结果显示，当前最先进的大型语言模型在大多数任务中失败，即使是推理型LLM在复杂设置下仍然失败。这表明现有LLM在处理结构化约束推理方面存在根本性局限。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM在处理结构化约束推理方面存在关键性缺陷。当前最先进的大型语言模型在大多数任务中失败，即使是最先进的推理型LLM在复杂场景下也无法胜任。这项工作为开发能够处理实际电网优化问题的LLM助手提供了严格的测试环境。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated great capabilities across diverse natural language tasks; yet their ability to solve abstraction and optimization problems with constraints remains scarcely explored. In this paper, we investigate whether LLMs can reason and optimize under the physical and operational constraints of Optimal Power Flow (OPF) problem. We introduce a challenging evaluation setup that requires a set of fundamental skills such as reasoning, structured input handling, arithmetic, and constrained optimization. Our evaluation reveals that SoTA LLMs fail in most of the tasks, and that reasoning LLMs still fail in the most complex settings. Our findings highlight critical gaps in LLMs' ability to handle structured reasoning under constraints, and this work provides a rigorous testing environment for developing more capable LLM assistants that can tackle real-world power grid optimization problems.

</details>


### [182] [MedCausalX: Adaptive Causal Reasoning with Self-Reflection for Trustworthy Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23085)
*Jianxin Lin,Chunzheng Zhu,Peter J. Kneuertz,Yunfei Bai,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedCausalX is an end-to-end framework for causally grounded medical reasoning in Vision-Language Models. It addresses three core challenges in medical CoT reasoning by introducing the CRMed dataset with anatomical annotations and counterfactual variants, and employing a two-stage adaptive reflection architecture with causal/verify tokens, refined by trajectory-level causal correction via error-attributed RL.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing medical chain-of-thought (CoT) models lack explicit mechanisms to represent and enforce causal reasoning, making them vulnerable to spurious correlations and limiting clinical reliability. Three core challenges exist: adaptively triggering causal correction, constructing high-quality causal-spurious contrastive samples, and maintaining causal consistency across reasoning trajectories.

Method: MedCausalX employs a two-stage adaptive reflection architecture with ⟨causal⟩ and ⟨verify⟩ tokens, enabling autonomous causal analysis and verification. Additionally, a trajectory-level causal correction objective optimized through error-attributed reinforcement learning refines reasoning chains to distinguish genuine causal dependencies from shortcut associations.

Result: Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks show that MedCausalX consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, improving diagnostic consistency by +5.4 points, reducing hallucination by over 10 points, and attaining top spatial grounding IoU.

Conclusion: MedCausalX sets a new standard for causally grounded medical reasoning by improving diagnostic consistency by +5.4 points, reducing hallucination by over 10 points, and achieving top spatial grounding IoU through explicit causal reasoning modeling in medical VLMs.

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled interpretable medical diagnosis by integrating visual perception with linguistic reasoning. Yet, existing medical chain-of-thought (CoT) models lack explicit mechanisms to represent and enforce causal reasoning, leaving them vulnerable to spurious correlations and limiting their clinical reliability. We pinpoint three core challenges in medical CoT reasoning: how to adaptively trigger causal correction, construct high-quality causal-spurious contrastive samples, and maintain causal consistency across reasoning trajectories. To address these challenges, we propose MedCausalX, an end-to-end framework explicitly models causal reasoning chains in medical VLMs. We first introduce the CRMed dataset providing fine-grained anatomical annotations, structured causal reasoning chains, and counterfactual variants that guide the learning of causal relationships beyond superficial correlations. Building upon CRMed, MedCausalX employs a two-stage adaptive reflection architecture equipped with $\langle$causal$\rangle$ and $\langle$verify$\rangle$ tokens, enabling the model to autonomously determine when and how to perform causal analysis and verification. Finally, a trajectory-level causal correction objective optimized through error-attributed reinforcement learning refines the reasoning chain, allowing the model to distinguish genuine causal dependencies from shortcut associations. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks show that MedCausalX consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, improving diagnostic consistency by +5.4 points, reducing hallucination by over 10 points, and attaining top spatial grounding IoU, thereby setting a new standard for causally grounded medical reasoning.

</details>


### [183] [Between Rules and Reality: On the Context Sensitivity of LLM Moral Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23114)
*Adrian Sauter,Mona Schirmer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构建了Contextual MoralChoice数据集，系统研究22个LLM在道德困境中的语境敏感性，发现模型与人类被不同语境因素触发，且基础对齐不等于语境敏感性对齐，并提出激活转向方法来控制模型的语境敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类道德决策高度依赖情境，但LLM道德研究主要考察固定情境。该研究旨在填补这一空白，探索LLM在不同语境下的道德判断表现。

Method: 构建了Contextual MoralChoice数据集，包含系统性的语境变化（功利主义、情感性、关系性），基于道德心理学中已知会改变人类判断的语境因素。对22个LLM进行评估，并与人类调查进行对比。

Result: 1) 几乎所有模型都表现出语境敏感性，会改变其判断倾向向违规行为；2) 模型和人类被不同的语境变化因素触发；3) 在基础情况下与人类判断对齐的模型不一定在语境敏感性方面也对齐。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一种激活转向(activation steering)方法，可以可靠地增加或减少模型的语境敏感性。研究表明，在基础情况下与人类判断对齐的模型不一定在语境敏感性方面也对齐，这引发了对控制LLM语境敏感性的思考。

Abstract: A human's moral decision depends heavily on the context. Yet research on LLM morality has largely studied fixed scenarios. We address this gap by introducing Contextual MoralChoice, a dataset of moral dilemmas with systematic contextual variations known from moral psychology to shift human judgment: consequentialist, emotional, and relational. Evaluating 22 LLMs, we find that nearly all models are context-sensitive, shifting their judgments toward rule-violating behavior. Comparing with a human survey, we find that models and humans are most triggered by different contextual variations, and that a model aligned with human judgments in the base case is not necessarily aligned in its contextual sensitivity. This raises the question of controlling contextual sensitivity, which we address with an activation steering approach that can reliably increase or decrease a model's contextual sensitivity.

</details>


### [184] [Describe-Then-Act: Proactive Agent Steering via Distilled Language-Action World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23149)
*Massimiliano Pappa,Luca Romani,Valentino Sacco,Alessio Palma,Stéphane Lathuilière,Fabio Galasso,Xavier Alameda-Pineda,Indro Spinelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DILLO是一种无需视觉模拟的快速世界模型，通过潜在状态+动作的语义预测实现失败预防，比视觉生成方法快14倍，成功率提升达15个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前依赖视觉模拟的世界模型方法产生极高延迟（每步数秒），在部署安全关键智能体时面临不可接受的计算成本。

Method: 提出DILLO（DIstiLLed Language-ActiOn World Model），一种快速引导层，范式从"simulate-then-act"转变为"describe-then-act"。通过跨模态蒸馏训练：特权视觉语言模型教师标注离线轨迹，潜在条件大语言模型学生学习预测语义结果。

Result: 实现14倍加速；DILLO能生成高保真下一状态描述并引导策略，在MetaWorld上成功率提升最高15个百分点，在LIBERO上平均提升9.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了视觉处理对于失败预防并非必要，训练策略的潜在状态结合计划动作已足以预测结果。DILLO通过文本-only推理路径完全绕过视觉生成，实现14倍加速，在MetaWorld和LIBERO任务中将成功率分别提升最多15个百分点和平均9.3个百分点。

Abstract: Deploying safety-critical agents requires anticipating the consequences of actions before they are executed. While world models offer a paradigm for this proactive foresight, current approaches relying on visual simulation incur prohibitive latencies, often exceeding several seconds per step. In this work, we challenge the assumption that visual processing is necessary for failure prevention. We show that a trained policy's latent state, combined with its planned actions, already encodes sufficient information to anticipate action outcomes, making visual simulation redundant for failure prevention. To this end, we introduce DILLO (DIstiLLed Language-ActiOn World Model), a fast steering layer that shifts the paradigm from "simulate-then-act" to "describe-then-act." DILLO is trained via cross-modal distillation, where a privileged Vision Language Model teacher annotates offline trajectories and a latent-conditioned Large Language Model student learns to predict semantic outcomes. This creates a text-only inference path, bypassing heavy visual generation entirely, achieving a 14x speedup over baselines. Experiments on MetaWorld and LIBERO demonstrate that DILLO produces high-fidelity descriptions of the next state and is able to steer the policy, improving episode success rate by up to 15 pp and 9.3 pp on average across tasks.

</details>


### [185] [PERMA: Benchmarking Personalized Memory Agents via Event-Driven Preference and Realistic Task Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23231)
*Shuochen Liu,Junyi Zhu,Long Shu,Junda Lin,Yuhao Chen,Haotian Zhang,Chao Zhang,Derong Xu,Jia Li,Bo Tang,Zhiyu Li,Feiyu Xiong,Enhong Chen,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PERMA是一个评估大语言模型长期记忆中角色一致性的基准，解决了传统评估忽略用户偏好随时间演变的问题。包含文本变异性和语言对齐以模拟真实世界数据特征，实验表明高级记忆系统虽有优势但在跨时间跨域场景下仍需改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法将偏好相关对话与无关对话交错，本质上等同于"大海捞针"式的检索任务，忽略了驱动用户偏好演变的事件间关系。现实世界的个性化有一个基本特征：偏好是在嘈杂上下文中逐渐浮现并跨交互累积的。

Method: 设计了PERMA基准测试，包含多选和交互两种任务形式探测模型对时间轴上角色的理解。基准包含跨多会话和多领域的时序交互事件，插入偏好相关查询。创新点包括(1)文本变异性和(2)语言对齐来模拟真实世界的嘈杂用户输入和个体习语。

Result: 实验表明，通过关联相关交互，高级记忆系统能提取更精确的用户偏好并减少token消耗，优于传统的原始对话语义检索。然而，这些系统仍难以在时间深度和跨域干扰下保持连贯的角色设定。

Conclusion: 当前模型在跨时间跨领域的角色一致性维护上仍存在显著不足，需要更强大的个性化记忆管理系统。高级记忆系统通过关联相关交互可以提取更精确的用户偏好，减少token消耗，但仍难以在时间深度和跨域干扰下保持连贯的角色设定。

Abstract: Empowering large language models with long-term memory is crucial for building agents that adapt to users' evolving needs. However, prior evaluations typically interleave preference-related dialogues with irrelevant conversations, reducing the task to needle-in-a-haystack retrieval while ignoring relationships between events that drive the evolution of user preferences. Such settings overlook a fundamental characteristic of real-world personalization: preferences emerge gradually and accumulate across interactions within noisy contexts. To bridge this gap, we introduce PERMA, a benchmark designed to evaluate persona consistency over time beyond static preference recall. Additionally, we incorporate (1) text variability and (2) linguistic alignment to simulate erratic user inputs and individual idiolects in real-world data. PERMA consists of temporally ordered interaction events spanning multiple sessions and domains, with preference-related queries inserted over time. We design both multiple-choice and interactive tasks to probe the model's understanding of persona along the interaction timeline. Experiments demonstrate that by linking related interactions, advanced memory systems can extract more precise preferences and reduce token consumption, outperforming traditional semantic retrieval of raw dialogues. Nevertheless, they still struggle to maintain a coherent persona across temporal depth and cross-domain interference, highlighting the need for more robust personalized memory management in agents. Our code and data are open-sourced at https://github.com/PolarisLiu1/PERMA.

</details>


### [186] [MemCollab: Cross-Agent Memory Collaboration via Contrastive Trajectory Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23234)
*Yurui Chang,Yiran Wu,Qingyun Wu,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对异构LLM智能体间记忆共享难题，提出MemCollab框架，通过对比不同智能体推理轨迹构建任务感知记忆，显著提升多智能体系统的准确率和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中记忆与特定智能体紧密耦合，在异构智能体部署场景下，直接转移记忆会导致性能下降，因为记忆纠缠了任务相关知识与智能体特定偏差。

Method: 1) 对比学习：通过对比不同智能体在同一任务上的推理轨迹，提炼共享的任务级不变量；2) 任务感知检索：根据任务类别条件化记忆访问，确保推理时只检索相关约束。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准测试中，MemCollab一致性地提升了准确率和推理时效率，跨模态族设置下同样有效。

Conclusion: MemCollab通过对比不同智能体在相同任务上的推理轨迹，提炼出抽象的推理约束（共享的任务级不变量），同时抑制智能体特定的偏差。任务感知检索机制确保只使用相关的约束。实验表明，协作构建的记忆可作为不同LLM智能体的共享推理资源，在数学推理和代码生成任务上显著提升准确率和推理效率，且适用于跨模态族设置。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents rely on memory mechanisms to reuse knowledge from past problem-solving experiences. Existing approaches typically construct memory in a per-agent manner, tightly coupling stored knowledge to a single model's reasoning style. In modern deployments with heterogeneous agents, a natural question arises: can a single memory system be shared across different models? We found that naively transferring memory between agents often degrades performance, as such memory entangles task-relevant knowledge with agent-specific biases. To address this challenge, we propose MemCollab, a collaborative memory framework that constructs agent-agnostic memory by contrasting reasoning trajectories generated by different agents on the same task. This contrastive process distills abstract reasoning constraints that capture shared task-level invariants while suppressing agent-specific artifacts. We further introduce a task-aware retrieval mechanism that conditions memory access on task category, ensuring that only relevant constraints are used at inference time. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks demonstrate that MemCollab consistently improves both accuracy and inference-time efficiency across diverse agents, including cross-modal-family settings. Our results show that the collaboratively constructed memory can function as a shared reasoning resource for diverse LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [187] [Online library learning in human visual puzzle solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23244)
*Pinzhe Zhao,Emanuele Sansone,Marta Kryven,Bonan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究人类在视觉谜题任务中如何形成和使用可重用抽象（helpers）。通过在线实验和计算建模发现，人们会随经验调整抽象策略，从追求完整性转向追求效率，且程序诱导模型配合库学习比单纯程序长度更能预测人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当学习复杂新任务时，人们虽不确定未来需求，但仍会形成高效的可重用抽象来简化未来工作。该研究旨在探索这一过程的具体机制。

Method: 在线实验：参与者解决难度递增的视觉谜题任务，创建和重用名为"helpers"的中间构造来捕获重复结构。计算建模：使用程序诱导模型结合库学习来估计搜索空间，并将模型预测与人类决策时间和操作数量进行对比。

Result: 早期阶段：参与者创建大量helpers，优先考虑完整性而非效率。随着经验积累：helpers使用变得更具选择性和效率，反映出对重用机会和成本的敏感性。helpers的可用性使参与者能够解决原本困难或不可能解决的谜题。程序诱导模型+库学习能预测决策时间和操作数量；单纯程序长度只能预测失败，不能预测努力程度。

Conclusion: 人类问题解决中存在在线库学习机制，使人们能够灵活地构建、细化并重用抽象概念。 helpers的使用从早期注重完整性转变为后期注重效率，反映了对重用机会和成本的敏感性。程序诱导模型配合库学习比单纯程序长度更能预测任务努力和失败情况。

Abstract: When learning a novel complex task, people often form efficient reusable abstractions that simplify future work, despite uncertainty about the future. We study this process in a visual puzzle task where participants define and reuse helpers -- intermediate constructions that capture repeating structure. In an online experiment, participants solved puzzles of increasing difficulty. Early on, they created many helpers, favouring completeness over efficiency. With experience, helper use became more selective and efficient, reflecting sensitivity to reuse and cost. Access to helpers enabled participants to solve puzzles that were otherwise difficult or impossible. Computational modelling shows that human decision times and number of operations used to complete a puzzle increase with search space estimated by a program induction model with library learning. In contrast, raw program length predicts failure but not effort. Together, these results point to online library learning as a core mechanism in human problem solving, allowing people to flexibly build, refine, and reuse abstractions as task demands grow.

</details>


### [188] [LLM Olympiad: Why Model Evaluation Needs a Sealed Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23292)
*Jan Christian Blaise Cruz,Alham Fikri Aji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM时代基准测试容易被误导的问题，本文提议建立奥林匹克式评估活动，通过问题密封、提交冻结、标准化评测和结果公开等机制，使模型高性能更难被制造且更值得信赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NLP领域基准测试和排行榜容易产生误导：分数可能反映的是针对基准的优化、隐藏的评估选择或测试内容意外泄露，而非真实的通用能力。封闭式基准虽能延迟部分问题，但降低了透明度。

Method: 提出举办奥林匹克式评估活动：问题在评估前密封、提交物提前冻结、通过标准化评测框架统一运行所有参赛模型。评分后完全公开任务集和评测代码以实现可复现和审计。

Result: 提出了一种可行的奥林匹克式评估事件设计方案，兼顾了防止"作弊"和保持社区学习能力之间的平衡。

Conclusion: 该设计旨在使高性能更难被"制造"且更值得信赖。奥赛式评估作为封闭式基准测试的补充实践，在保持一定透明度的同时，增加了性能造假的难度。

Abstract: Benchmarks and leaderboards are how NLP most often communicates progress, but in the LLM era they are increasingly easy to misread. Scores can reflect benchmark-chasing, hidden evaluation choices, or accidental exposure to test content -- not just broad capability. Closed benchmarks delay some of these issues, but reduce transparency and make it harder for the community to learn from results. We argue for a complementary practice: an Olympiad-style evaluation event where problems are sealed until evaluation, submissions are frozen in advance, and all entries run through one standardized harness. After scoring, the full task set and evaluation code are released so results can be reproduced and audited. This design aims to make strong performance harder to ``manufacture'' and easier to trust.

</details>


### [189] [RelayS2S: A Dual-Path Speculative Generation for Real-Time Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23346)
*Long Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RelayS2S是一种混合语音对话架构，通过并行运行快速S2S模型起草响应前缀和慢速级联管道生成高质量延续，结合轻量级验证器门控切换，在实现与S2S相当的P90延迟的同时保持99%的级联响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端语音到语音(S2S)模型响应即时且自然处理轮次转换、反馈和中断，但输出语义较弱；级联管道(ASR->LLM)提供更强响应但延迟随模型规模增长，形成延迟与响应质量之间的根本矛盾。

Method: 提出混合架构RelayS2S，包含两条并行路径：快速路径使用双向S2S模型快速起草响应前缀并流式传输到TTS以实现低延迟音频起始，同时持续监听实时音频事件；慢速路径使用级联ASR->LLM管道基于已提交的前缀生成更高质量的延续；引入轻量级学习验证器门控切换过程，在适当时机提交前缀，或优雅地回退到仅使用慢速路径。

Result: 实验表明RelayS2S实现与S2S模型相当的P90起始延迟，同时在平均分数上保持99%的级联响应质量，且随着慢路径模型scale up，收益持续增长。

Conclusion: RelayS2S作为一种轻量级、即插即用的解决方案，可无缝集成到现有级联管道中，在保持低延迟的同时显著提升响应质量，且随着慢路径模型规模增大，收益愈发明显。

Abstract: Real-time spoken dialogue systems face a fundamental tension between latency and response quality. End-to-end speech-to-speech (S2S) models respond immediately and naturally handle turn-taking, backchanneling, and interruption, but produce semantically weaker outputs. Cascaded pipelines (ASR -> LLM) deliver stronger responses at the cost of latency that grows with model size. We present RelayS2S, a hybrid architecture that runs two paths in parallel upon turn detection. The fast path -- a duplex S2S model -- speculatively drafts a short response prefix that is streamed immediately to TTS for low-latency audio onset, while continuing to monitor live audio events. The slow path -- a cascaded ASR -> LLM pipeline -- generates a higher-quality continuation conditioned on the committed prefix, producing a seamless utterance. A lightweight learned verifier gates the handoff, committing the prefix when appropriate or falling back gracefully to the slow path alone. Experiments show that RelayS2S achieves P90 onset latency comparable to the S2S model while retaining 99% cascaded response quality in average score, with benefits growing as the slow-path model scales. Because the prefix handoff requires no architectural modification to either component, RelayS2S serves as a lightweight, drop-in addition to existing cascaded pipelines. Our code and data are publicly available at: https://github.com/mailong25/relays2s

</details>


### [190] [Beyond Preset Identities: How Agents Form Stances and Boundaries in Generative Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23406)
*Hanzhong Zhang,Siyang Song,Jindong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出混合方法框架研究LLM在多智能体社区中的立场形成与身份协商机制，提出IVB、说服敏感度和TAD三项新指标，发现内生立场覆盖预设身份、理性说服高效果、冲突性干预引发信任-行为解耦等关键现象，为动态对齐提供方法论基础


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽能模拟社会行为，但在复杂干预下的稳定立场形成和身份协商能力尚不明确，旨在克服静态评估的局限性

Method: 提出了一种结合计算虚拟民族志与定量社会认知分析的新颖混合方法框架，将人类研究者嵌入生成式多智能体社区，进行受控的话语干预以追踪集体认知的演变

Result: 1) 内生立场会覆盖预设身份，模型普遍表现出内在的进步偏见(IVB > 0)；2) 与立场一致的理性说服成功转变了90%的中立智能体并保持高度信任；3) 冲突性情绪挑衅在高级模型中引发40.0%的信任-行为解耦(TAD)率，伪善地改变立场同时报告低信任；4) 较小模型保持0%的TAD率，严格要求信任才能产生行为转变；5) 在共同立场指导下，智能体通过语言交互主动瓦解指定的权力层级并重建自组织社区边界

Conclusion: 这些发现暴露了静态提示工程的不稳定性，为人机混合社会中的动态对齐提供了方法论和定量基础

Abstract: While large language models simulate social behaviors, their capacity for stable stance formation and identity negotiation during complex interventions remains unclear. To overcome the limitations of static evaluations, this paper proposes a novel mixed-methods framework combining computational virtual ethnography with quantitative socio-cognitive profiling. By embedding human researchers into generative multiagent communities, controlled discursive interventions are conducted to trace the evolution of collective cognition. To rigorously measure how agents internalize and react to these specific interventions, this paper formalizes three new metrics: Innate Value Bias (IVB), Persuasion Sensitivity, and Trust-Action Decoupling (TAD). Across multiple representative models, agents exhibit endogenous stances that override preset identities, consistently demonstrating an innate progressive bias (IVB > 0). When aligned with these stances, rational persuasion successfully shifts 90% of neutral agents while maintaining high trust. In contrast, conflicting emotional provocations induce a paradoxical 40.0% TAD rate in advanced models, which hypocritically alter stances despite reporting low trust. Smaller models contrastingly maintain a 0% TAD rate, strictly requiring trust for behavioral shifts. Furthermore, guided by shared stances, agents use language interactions to actively dismantle assigned power hierarchies and reconstruct self organized community boundaries. These findings expose the fragility of static prompt engineering, providing a methodological and quantitative foundation for dynamic alignment in human-agent hybrid societies. The official code is available at: https://github.com/armihia/CMASE-Endogenous-Stances

</details>


### [191] [Bilevel Autoresearch: Meta-Autoresearching Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23420)
*Yaonan Qu,Meng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Bilevel Autoresearch提出一种双层框架，外层循环通过运行时生成新搜索机制代码来自主元优化内层autoresearch循环。在GPT预训练任务上，外层循环相比标准内层循环性能提升5倍，且成功自主发现了来自多个领域的优化机制，无需人类预先指定。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有所有autoresearch系统都是由人类阅读代码、识别瓶颈并编写新代码来改进的。作者提出问题：LLM是否可以自主完成同样的优化过程？核心思想是：如果autoresearch可以元优化自身，理论上可以元优化任何具有可测量目标的任务。



Method: 提出Bilevel Autoresearch框架：内层循环优化任务，外层循环通过运行时生成和注入新的搜索机制作为Python代码来元优化内层循环。两个循环使用相同的LLM，不需要更强的模型在元级别。外层循环自主发现优化机制，无需人类指定要探索的领域。



Result: 在Karpathy的GPT预训练基准上，元autoresearch外层循环相比标准内层循环实现了5倍的提升（-0.045 vs -0.009 val_bpb）。参数级调整 without 机制改变没有产生可靠增益。外层循环自主发现了来自组合优化、多臂老虎机和实验设计领域的机制，这些机制通过打破内层循环的确定性搜索模式、强制探索LLM先验系统性地避免的方向而取得成功。



Conclusion: Bilevel Autoresearch通过双层循环架构证明了元优化可以自主改进搜索机制，无需人类干预。实验表明外层循环在GPT预训练基准上比标准内层循环提升5倍（-0.045 vs -0.009 val_bpb）。该框架成功自主发现了组合优化、多臂老虎机和实验设计等领域的方法，这些机制通过打破内层循环的确定性搜索模式来发挥作用。作者认为这一原则可推广到任何具有可测量目标的任务。



Abstract: If autoresearch is itself a form of research, then autoresearch can be applied to research itself. We take this idea literally: we use an autoresearch loop to optimize the autoresearch loop. Every existing autoresearch system -- from Karpathy's single-track loop to AutoResearchClaw's multi-batch extension and EvoScientist's persistent memory -- was improved by a human who read the code, identified a bottleneck, and wrote new code. We ask whether an LLM can do the same, autonomously. We present Bilevel Autoresearch, a bilevel framework where an outer loop meta-optimizes the inner autoresearch loop by generating and injecting new search mechanisms as Python code at runtime. The inner loop optimizes the task; the outer loop optimizes how the inner loop searches. Both loops use the same LLM -- no stronger model is needed at the meta level. On Karpathy's GPT pretraining benchmark, the meta-autoresearch outer loop achieves a 5x improvement over the standard inner loop alone (-0.045 vs. -0.009 val_bpb), while parameter-level adjustment without mechanism change yields no reliable gain. The outer loop autonomously discovers mechanisms from combinatorial optimization, multi-armed bandits, and design of experiments -- without human specification of which domains to explore. These mechanisms succeed by breaking the inner loop's deterministic search patterns, forcing exploration of directions the LLM's priors systematically avoid. The core principle is simple: if autoresearch can meta-autoresearch itself, it can, in principle, meta-autoresearch anything with a measurable objective.

</details>


### [192] [Mecha-nudges for Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23433)
*Giulio Frey,Kawin Ethayarajh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究者提出"机械助推"概念——通过改变选择呈现方式系统影响AI智能体，同时不降低人类决策环境质量。结合贝叶斯说服框架与V可用信息建立统一测量尺度，并应用于Etsy平台案例，发现ChatGPT发布后商品列表的机器可用信息显著增加，为机械助推现象提供了实证证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体在人类环境中扮演越来越重要的角色，传统的"助推"(nudges)概念需要扩展。传统助推旨在影响人类决策而不限制选择或改变激励，但当AI也成为决策者时，环境可能被优化以影响AI，这可能间接影响人类决策环境。研究者认为需要正式定义和测量这种新现象。

Method: 该研究采用理论建模与实证分析相结合的方法。理论层面，研究者将贝叶斯说服框架（用于分析信息呈现如何影响决策）与V可用信息（一种观察者相对的信息论度量）相结合，建立统一的量化框架。实证层面，研究者选取Etsy平台作为案例，通过比较ChatGPT发布前后的商品列表文本特征变化，测量机器可用信息的改变。

Result: ChatGPT发布后，Etsy平台上的商品列表包含了显著更多的机器可用信息（关于产品选择的信息）。这一变化与系统性机械助推的假设一致，表明平台可能正在调整信息呈现方式以更好地被AI智能体（如ChatGPT）理解和利用。

Conclusion: 该研究首次正式化了"机械助推"(mecha-nudges)这一概念，表明AI智能体与人类共存的环境中，选项呈现方式可以被系统性地优化以影响AI行为，而不会损害人类决策环境。研究为理解AI对人类决策环境的影响提供了新的理论框架和测量工具。

<parameter name="method">结合贝叶斯说服框架(Bayesian persuasion framework)与V可用信息(V-usable information)，建立了一个统一的测量尺度——"可用信息比特"(bits of usable information)，用于比较不同干预措施、上下文和模型。以Etsy平台商品列表为案例，通过比较ChatGPT发布前后的商品列表信息变化进行实证分析。

<parameter name="motivation">随着AI智能体越来越多地在与人类相同的环境中做出决策，选项的呈现方式可能被同时优化用于机器和人类。传统 nudges 概念需要扩展，以解释这种AI与人类共存的新现象。作者提出问题：如何量化 choices presentation 对AI agents的影响，以及如何确保这种影响不会损害人类决策环境。

<parameter name="result">研究发现，ChatGPT发布后，Etsy商品列表中关于产品选择的机器可用信息显著增加，符合系统性机械助推的特征。这一发现表明，平台可能正在系统性地调整信息呈现方式以优化AI智能体的行为。

<parameter name="tldr">本文提出"机械助推"(mecha-nudges)概念——一种在不影响人类决策环境的前提下系统影响AI智能体选择呈现方式的变化。研究者结合贝叶斯说服框架与V可用信息理论建立统一测量尺度，并将该框架应用于Etsy平台，发现ChatGPT发布后商品列表的机器可用信息显著增加，为系统性机械助推提供了实证证据。

Abstract: Nudges are subtle changes to the way choices are presented to human decision-makers (e.g., opt-in vs. opt-out by default) that shift behavior without restricting options or changing incentives. As AI agents increasingly make decisions in the same environments as humans, the presentation of choices may be optimized for machines as well as people. We introduce mecha-nudges: changes to how choices are presented that systematically influence AI agents without degrading the decision environment for humans. To formalize mecha-nudges, we combine the Bayesian persuasion framework with V-usable information, a generalization of Shannon information that is observer-relative. This yields a common scale (bits of usable information) for comparing a wide range of interventions, contexts, and models. Applying our framework to product listings on Etsy -- a global marketplace for independent sellers -- we find that following ChatGPT's release, listings have significantly more machine-usable information about product selection, consistent with systematic mecha-nudging.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Interactive and Urgent HPC: State of the Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22542)
*Albert Reuther,William Arndt,Johannes Blaschke,Christian Boehme,Nick Brown,Antony Chazapis,Bjoern Enders,Jens Henrik Goebbert,Robert Henschel,Julian Kunkel,Maxime Martinasso,Michael Ringenburg,Rollin Thomas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了高性能计算(HPC)中交互性和紧急性的发展趋势，概述了将交互式和紧急HPC集成到现有生态系统的进展与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能手机、电子商务、协作平台和LLMs等应用的普及，用户与计算机的交互越来越趋向于交互式和紧急模式。HPC领域也出现了类似趋势，从模拟到数据分析再到机器学习的应用都需要更高的并行计算能力和更好的交互性。

Method: 本章节概述了迄今为止的进展，并展望了未来发展方向，探讨如何将交互式和紧急HPC的政策、技术和工具更好地集成到现有HPC生态系统中。

Result: 概述了HPC在交互式和紧急计算方面已取得的进展，并提出了未来发展的主要方向和路径。

Conclusion: 需要将交互式和紧急HPC策略、技术更深度地整合到HPC生态系统中，以满足现代应用对并行计算能力和交互性的需求。

Abstract: When we think of how we use smartphones, e-commerce, collaboration platforms, LLMs, etc., most of our interactions with computers are interactive and often urgent. Similar trends of interactivity and urgency are coming to HPC, with applications from simulations to data analysis and machine learning requiring more parallel computational capability and more interactivity. This chapter overviews the progress made so far along with some vectors of what the path forward will bring for greater integration of interactive and urgent HPC policies, techniques, and technologies into our HPC ecosystems.

</details>


### [194] [Rank-Aware Resource Scheduling for Tightly-Coupled MPI Workloads on Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22691)
*Tianfang Xie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究了在Kubernetes上为CFD求解器实现rank感知的细粒度CPU配置，发现使用请求式CPU分配（避免硬限制）可消除性能惩罚，实现20%性能提升并减少82%的低负载子域CPU配置，显著提升云集群的packing效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对共享云托管集群，探讨如何通过细粒度CPU配置减少低负载子域的资源预留，在不显著降低性能的前提下提升集群packing效率。

Method: 在Kubernetes上实现了rank感知的资源调度，将每个MPI rank映射到CPU请求与其子域单元数成比例的pod；并演示了Kubernetes v1.35 GA的原地Pod垂直扩缩容功能，用于模拟中期的CPU调整。实验在AWS EC2 c5.xlarge集群（4-16 ranks）上运行k3s v1.35，使用101K单元网格。

Result: 1) 使用Linux CFS带宽控制器的硬CPU限制会导致MPI_Allreduce屏障级联 stall，造成78倍 slowdown；而仅使用请求式分配可完全消除节流；2) 在不可突发的c5.xlarge实例上，相同CPU的同心分解比Scotch基线快19%，加上比例CPU配置进一步提升3%；3) 在16个MPI ranks和101K单元网格上，比例分配比等量分配快20%，同时将稀疏子域的已配置CPU降低82%，释放6.5 vCPU调度余量。

Conclusion: 通过请求式CPU分配（而非硬限制）可以完全消除CPU节流，实现20%的性能提升，同时将低负载子域的CPU配置降低82%，释放6.5个vCPU的调度余量，证明了细粒度CPU配置在云托管集群中可显著提升集群packing效率。

Abstract: Fully provisioned Message Passing Interface (MPI) parallelism achieves near-optimal wall-clock time for Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) solvers. This work addresses a complementary question for shared, cloud-managed clusters: can fine-grained CPU provisioning reduce resource reservation of low-load subdomains, improving cluster packing efficiency without unacceptably degrading performance?
  We propose rank-aware resource scheduling on Kubernetes, mapping each MPI rank to a pod whose CPU request is proportional to its subdomain cell count. We also demonstrate In-Place Pod Vertical Scaling (Kubernetes v1.35 GA) for mid-simulation CPU adjustment without pod restart.
  Three findings emerge. First, hard CPU limits via the Linux CFS bandwidth controller cause 78x slowdown through cascading stalls at MPI_Allreduce barriers; requests-only allocation eliminates throttling entirely. Second, on non-burstable c5.xlarge instances, concentric decomposition with equal CPU is 19% faster than the Scotch baseline, while adding proportional CPU yields a further 3% improvement. Third, at 16 MPI ranks on 101K-cell meshes, proportional allocation is 20% faster than equal allocation while reducing sparse-subdomain provisioned CPU by 82%, freeing 6.5 vCPU of scheduling headroom.
  Experiments are conducted on AWS EC2 c5.xlarge clusters (4-16 ranks) running k3s v1.35. All scripts and data are released as open source.

</details>


### [195] [PCR: A Prefetch-Enhanced Cache Reuse System for Low-Latency RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23049)
*Wenfeng Wang,Xiaofeng Hou,Peng Tang,Hengyi Zhou,Jing Wang,Xinkai Wang,Chao Li,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PCR是一种通过智能预取和流水线数据移动提升KV-cache复用效率的系统，针对RAG系统中长序列输入导致的预填充延迟问题，提出前缀树缓存+前瞻LRU、层间CUDA流重叠、队列预取三种技术，最终实现最高2.47倍的TTFT加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中外部文档集成导致输入序列过长，在预填充阶段产生显著的计算成本，特别是在高吞吐量服务场景下。KV-cache复用虽能解决这一问题，但面临缓存命中率低、CPU-GPU数据传输开销大、SSD I/O速度慢三大 Practical 挑战。

Method: 提出三种关键技术：(1)前缀树缓存结构结合前瞻LRU替换策略，利用调度器队列中的待处理请求提升缓存命中率；(2)层间重叠技术，通过CUDA流流水线化KV-cache加载和GPU计算以隐藏通信延迟；(3)基于队列的预取机制，在需要前主动将KV-cache从SSD加载到DRAM。

Result: 实验表明，PCR在平均TTFT上优于现有KV-cache复用方法，最高实现2.47倍加速。

Conclusion: PCR通过智能预取和流水线数据移动显著提升了KV-cache复用效率，在TTFT指标上实现了最高2.47倍的加速，为高吞吐量RAG服务场景提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems enhance the performance of large language models (LLMs) by incorporating supplementary retrieved documents, enabling more accurate and context-aware responses. However, integrating these external documents often results in very long input sequences, which significantly increases computation costs during the prefill stage, where key-value (KV) representations for all input tokens are generated. This latency bottleneck becomes especially pronounced under high-throughput serving scenarios. KV-cache reuse offers a promising solution by storing previously computed KV states for shared input prefixes, thereby avoiding redundant computation across requests that contain overlapping context. Yet, the effectiveness of cache reuse is often limited by three practical challenges: low cache hit rates due to naive eviction policies, high CPU-GPU data transfer overhead, and slow SSD I/O when caches spill to storage. To address these issues, we propose PCR, a system designed to maximize KV-cache reuse efficiency through intelligent prefetching and pipelined data movement. Specifically, PCR introduces three key techniques: (1) a prefix-tree caching structure with a look-ahead LRU replacement policy that uses pending requests in the scheduler queue to improve cache hit ratios; (2) layer-wise overlapping that pipelines KV-cache loading and GPU computation across CUDA streams to hide communication latency; and (3) queue-based prefetching that proactively loads relevant KV caches from SSD into DRAM before they are needed. Extensive experiments show that PCR outperforms existing KV-cache reuse methods, achieving up to a 2.47x speedup in terms of average TTFT.

</details>


### [196] [Communication-Aware Diffusion Load Balancing for Persistently Interacting Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23329)
*Maya Taylor,Kavitha Chandrasekar,Laxmikant V. Kale*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用应用通信图的分布式扩散负载均衡算法，针对通信密集型应用，在减少跨节点通信的同时实现有效负载分配，包含通信模式不可用时的算法变体，并在Perlmutter上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有不规则和时变工作负载的并行应用经常遭受负载不平衡问题，现有的动态负载均衡技术难以有效应对通信密集型应用的特殊挑战。

Method: 提出了一种针对通信密集型应用的新型分布式扩散负载均衡算法，通过利用应用通信图来减少跨节点通信，同时有效分配负载。还提出了通信模式不可用时的算法变体，并进行了算法优化。

Result: 在NERSC的Perlmutter超级计算机上最多8个节点进行了Particle-in-Cell基准测试，并与其他相关负载均衡策略进行了模拟对比。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效解决通信密集型应用的负载平衡问题，在保持负载均衡的同时显著减少跨节点通信量，为大规模并行计算提供了新的负载均衡策略。

Abstract: Parallel applications with irregular and time-varying workloads often suffer from load imbalance. Dynamic load balancing techniques address this challenge by redistributing work during execution. We present a new type of distributed diffusion-based load balancing targeted at communication-intensive applications with persistently communicating objects. Leveraging the application's communication graph, our strategy reduces across-node communication while simultaneously distributing load effectively. We also propose an algorithmic variant for cases where the communication patterns are not readily available. We explore optimizations to our algorithm, and comparisons with other related load balancing strategies in simulation and on a Particle-in-Cell benchmark on up to 8 nodes of Perlmutter at NERSC.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [SCALE-Sim TPU: Validating and Extending SCALE-Sim for TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22535)
*Jingtian Dang,Ritik Raj,Changhai Man,Jianming Tong,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SCALE-Sim TPU是一个针对TPU风格加速器的经验证和扩展的cycle-accurate模拟器，通过TPU v4硬件验证、learned延迟模型和StableHLO前端集成三个贡献，显著提升了systolic accelerator仿真的准确性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有cycle-accurate simulators在systolic accelerators研究中存在两个关键问题：(1) 与真实硬件的验证不足；(2) 与现代ML编译器栈的集成较差。这些局限性影响了模拟结果的准确性和实用性。

Method: 1. 对SCALE-Sim的systolic GEMM模型进行TPU v4硬件实测验证；2. 引入轻量级学习延迟模型估算非systolic elementwise操作延迟；3. 集成StableHLO前端以支持JAX和PyTorch工作负载的直接仿真。

Result: (1) 模拟cycle计数与TPU v4硬件延迟呈现强线性相关，可实现简单的cycle-to-latency映射；(2) 学习延迟模型仅使用tensor size和shape即可达到低于3%的中位相对误差，显著改善了端到端延迟估算；(3) StableHLO前端可统一通过编译器IR直接模拟来自JAX和PyTorch的工作负载。

Conclusion: SCALE-Sim TPU通过三方面的贡献提升了TPU架构的cycle-accurate模拟保真度、覆盖范围和实用性，为TPU风格加速器的全模型性能分析提供了更可靠的仿真工具。

Abstract: Cycle-accurate simulators are widely used to study systolic accelerators, yet their accuracy and usability are often limited by weak validation against real hardware and poor integration with modern ML compiler stacks. This paper presents SCALE-Sim TPU, a validated and extended version of SCALE-Sim v3 for TPU-style accelerators. Specifically, we make three contributions: (1) We validate SCALE-Sim's systolic GEMM model against measurements on Google TPU v4 and show that simulated cycle counts exhibit a strong linear correlation with hardware latency, enabling a simple cycle-to-latency mapping. (2) We introduce lightweight learned latency models for non-systolic elementwise operations, achieving median relative errors below 3 percent using only tensor size and shape, substantially improving end-to-end latency estimation. (3) We integrate a StableHLO-based frontend that allows workloads from modern ML frameworks such as JAX and PyTorch to be simulated directly via a unified compiler IR. Together, these contributions improve the fidelity, coverage, and practicality of cycle-accurate simulation for whole-model performance analysis on TPUs.

</details>


### [198] [CPU Simulation with Ranked Set Sampling and Repeated Subsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22598)
*Magnus Ekman*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文提出将统计文献中成熟的排序集抽样(RSS)技术应用于计算机体系结构模拟，通过重复子采样方案识别代表性模拟区域。实验表明该方法可将置信区间宽度降低50%，最大误差从35%降至10%，在SPEC CPU 2017基准测试中实现平均误差低于2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机系统模拟研究通常依赖于执行有限数量的短应用区域，因为完整的端到端模拟非常耗时。为保持代表性，现有方法采用随机抽样或基于阶段的特征识别方法。该研究重新审视随机抽样在计算机架构模拟中的适用性，旨在评估不同微架构配置下置信水平的变化，并寻找更有效的抽样方法。

Method: 研究首先分析样本标准差与样本均值的相关性，评估不同微架构配置下的置信水平变化。然后采用统计文献中成熟的排序集抽样(RSS)技术进行采样，并引入重复子采样方案来识别代表性模拟区域。研究评估了两种选择标准以比较不同子样本选择策略的准确性。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) RSS技术相比简单随机抽样可将置信区间宽度降低高达50%；(2) 重复子采样方案对于固定样本量可将最大观测误差从35%降至10%；(3) 更明智的子样本选择策略可带来额外的精度提升；(4) 在模拟30个100万指令区域的场景下，该方法在SPEC CPU 2017整数应用中实现平均误差低于2%、最大误差3.5%。

Conclusion: 研究提出的RSS方法和重复子采样方案能够显著提升计算机体系结构模拟的准确性和效率。RSS技术可将置信区间宽度降低50%，重复子采样方案将最大观测误差从35%降至10%。该方法在SPEC CPU 2017整数基准测试中实现了平均误差低于2%、最大误差3.5%的精度，为未来计算机架构模拟研究提供了更可靠的区域选择框架。

Abstract: Computer system simulation studies routinely rely on executing a limited number of short application regions, since full end-to-end simulation is prohibitively time-consuming. To preserve representativeness, existing methods employ either random sampling or phase-based characterization to identify representative regions.
  In this work, we revisit random sampling in the context of computer architecture simulation. To assess how the confidence level varies with different micro-architectural configurations, we examine how the sample standard deviation relates to the sample mean. We show that the ranked set sampling (RSS) technique - well established in the statistical literature - maps naturally to architectural simulation and yields significantly tighter confidence intervals than simple random sampling. Across our experiments, RSS reduces the confidence interval width by up to 50%.
  We further introduce a repeated subsampling scheme that identifies representative simulation regions for future studies. For a fixed sample size, this approach reduces the maximum observed error from 35% to 10%. Evaluating two selection criteria, we find that more informed subsample selection provides additional accuracy gains. Overall, our method achieves an average error below 2% and a maximum error of 3.5% across individual SPEC CPU 2017 Integer applications when simulating 30 regions of 1 million instructions each.

</details>


### [199] [CPU Simulation Using Two-Phase Stratified Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22605)
*Magnus Ekman*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视了SPEC CPU 2017整数套件中的程序相位行为，揭示传统SimPoint方法在使用典型采样点数量时可能产生40-60%的性能预测误差。通过将SimPoint转化为分层采样问题并提出两阶段采样方案，研究将最大误差降至3%，同时将样本量需求降低一个数量级，为统计严谨的架构仿真提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整端到端应用执行在计算机架构研究中耗时过长，SimPoint作为行业标准解决方案通过选择少量代表性代码区域来捕捉程序相位行为，但需要评估这种相位行为的显著程度以及典型SimPoint使用可达到的准确性。

Method: 1) 将SimPoint重新表述为分层采样问题，推导保守置信区间；2) 将SimPoint分解为分层（聚类）和样本单元选择（质心选择）两个基本组成部分，分析其各自对准确性的影响；3) 提出两阶段（双）采样方案：先用大样本进行初步随机采样以改善分层，再进行更具代表性的区域选择。

Result: 1) 常见SimPoint数量会引入 substantial estimation errors；2) 经验分析确认：使用20个SimPoints时，两个应用程序表现出40-60%的性能预测误差；3) 两阶段采样方案将最大per-application error降至3%；4) 相比简单随机采样，所提方法维持tight analytical confidence interval的同时将所需样本量降低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 研究证明了传统SimPoint方法在使用20个采样点时可能导致40-60%的性能预测误差。通过将SimPoint重新解释为分层采样问题，并采用两阶段（双）采样方案，可将每应用程序的最大误差降至3%。该方法相比简单随机采样维持tight confidence interval，同时将样本量降低一个数量级，为统计上严谨且高效的架构仿真提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Simulation remains a cornerstone of computer architecture research, yet full end-to-end application execution is prohibitively time-consuming. The industry-standard solution, SimPoint, mitigates this cost by selecting a small number of representative code regions that capture program phase behavior. In this work, we take a fresh look at phase behavior in the SPEC CPU 2017 Integer suite to assess how pronounced such behavior truly is and what accuracy can be expected from typical SimPoint usage. Based on previously published data, we argue that common SimPoint counts can induce substantial estimation errors. To explore this further, we recast SimPoint as a stratified sampling problem, which enables the derivation of a conservative confidence interval. The analysis indicates that significant errors are expected, and our empirical analysis confirms this: with 20 SimPoints, two applications exhibit 40-60% performance prediction error.
  We decompose SimPoint into its two fundamental components - stratification (clustering) and sample-unit selection (centroid choice) - and analyze their individual effects on accuracy. We then extend the approach into a two-phase (double) sampling scheme, in which a large preliminary random sample enables improved stratification and more representative region selection. Using this method, the maximum per-application error drops to 3%. Finally, we demonstrate that the proposed two-phase stratified framework achieves an order-of-magnitude reduction in required sample size compared to simple random sampling while maintaining a tight analytical confidence interval, suggesting a practical path toward statistically grounded and efficient architectural simulation.

</details>


### [200] [Characterizing CPU-Induced Slowdowns in Multi-GPU LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22774)
*Euijun Chung,Yuxiao Jia,Aaron Jezghani,Hyesoon Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 研究揭示了多GPU LLM推理中CPU诱导的性能瓶颈，发现即使在使用进程级分离和CUDA Graphs优化的情况下，CPU不足仍会导致内核延迟启动、通信停滞和标记化延迟增加。增加CPU核心可显著改善性能，TTFT延迟降低1.36-5.40倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多GPU系统的性能常常受到一个被忽视的组件限制：CPU。CPU无法保持GPU忙碌，导致GPU利用不足。

Method: 通过详细研究现代LLM推理和服务工作负载，系统性地分析了CPU诱导的性能下降。评估了采用进程级分离和CUDA Graphs等现代GPU侧优化的情况，并在中等服务负载下进行测试。

Result: CPU资源不足的配置经常超时，而提供足够的CPU资源可恢复响应能力并将TTFT延迟降低1.36-5.40倍，且无需额外的GPU。

Conclusion: CPU provisioning is a crucial factor in multi-GPU LLM inference configuration. Increasing CPU cores can substantially improve performance and stability at minimal additional cost, reducing TTFT latency by 1.36-5.40x without requiring additional GPUs.

Abstract: Large-scale machine learning workloads increasingly rely on multi-GPU systems, yet their performance is often limited by an overlooked component: the CPU. Through a detailed study of modern large language model (LLM) inference and serving workloads, we find that multi-GPU performance frequently degrades not because GPUs are saturated, but because CPUs fail to keep the GPUs busy. Under limited CPU allocations, systems exhibit symptoms such as delayed kernel launch, stalled communication, and increased tokenization latency, leading to severe GPU underutilization even when ample GPU resources are available. This work presents a systematic analysis of CPU-induced slowdowns in multi-GPU LLM inference. We show that these bottlenecks persist even in serving stacks that employ process-level separation and modern GPU-side optimizations such as CUDA Graphs. Since the marginal cost of additional CPU cores is small relative to GPU instance pricing, our evaluation indicates that increasing the number of CPU cores can substantially improve performance and stability at minimal additional cost. Under moderate serving load, we observe that CPU-starved configurations frequently time out, while providing adequate CPU resources restores responsiveness and reduces time-to-first-token (TTFT) latency by 1.36-5.40x across configurations, all without requiring additional GPUs. This work shows that CPU provisioning is a crucial factor in multi-GPU LLM inference configuration, helping prevent control-side bottlenecks.

</details>


### [201] [TRINE: A Token-Aware, Runtime-Adaptive FPGA Inference Engine for Multimodal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22867)
*Hyunwoo Oh,Hanning Chen,Sanggeon Yun,Yang Ni,Suyeon Jang,Behnam Khaleghi,Fei Wen,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: TRINE是一种单比特流FPGA加速器和编译器，通过统一层抽象（DDMM/SDDMM/SpMM）和模式可切换引擎实现端到端多模态推理，无需重新配置，在嵌入式平台上实现SOTA延迟和能效


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态堆栈混合ViTs、CNNs、GNNs和NLP transformer给嵌入式平台带来压力，计算/内存模式分歧，硬实时目标留有余量很少

Method: 将层统一为DDMM/SDDMM/SpMM操作，映射到可在运行时切换的模式可切换引擎（三种模式：权重/输出固定 systolic array、1xCS SIMD、可路由加法树RADT）；采用宽度匹配的两阶段top-k单元实现流内token剪枝；使用依赖感知层卸载（DALO）重叠独立内核

Result: 与RTX 4090相比延迟降低最高22.57倍；与Jetson Orin Nano（20-21W）相比延迟降低最高6.86倍；token剪枝在ViT重型流水线上最高带来7.8倍提升；DALO贡献最高79%吞吐量提升；int8量化精度损失<2.5%

Conclusion: TRINE在单个比特流中实现了统一视觉、语言和图工作负载的SOTA延迟和能效，int8量化精度损失<2.5%，在Alveo U50和ZCU104上验证，分别比RTX 4090快22.57倍，比Jetson Orin Nano快6.86倍

Abstract: Multimodal stacks that mix ViTs, CNNs, GNNs, and transformer NLP strain embedded platforms because their compute/memory patterns diverge and hard real-time targets leave little slack. TRINE is a single-bitstream FPGA accelerator and compiler that executes end-to-end multimodal inference without reconfiguration. Layers are unified as DDMM/SDDMM/SpMM and mapped to a mode-switchable engine that toggles at runtime among weight/output-stationary systolic, 1xCS SIMD, and a routable adder tree (RADT) on a shared PE array. A width-matched, two-stage top-k unit enables in-stream token pruning, while dependency-aware layer offloading (DALO) overlaps independent kernels across reconfigurable processing units to sustain utilization. Evaluated on Alveo U50 and ZCU104, TRINE reduces latency by up to 22.57x vs. RTX 4090 and 6.86x vs. Jetson Orin Nano at 20-21 W; token pruning alone yields up to 7.8x on ViT-heavy pipelines, and DALO contributes up to 79% throughput improvement. With int8 quantization, accuracy drops remain <2.5% across representative tasks, delivering state-of-the-art latency and energy efficiency for unified vision, language, and graph workloads-in one bitstream.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Beyond Hard Constraints: Budget-Conditioned Reachability For Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22292)
*Janaka Chathuranga Brahmanage,Akshat Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离线安全强化学习中累积成本约束难以处理的问题，提出安全条件可达性集合来解耦奖励与安全约束，无需拉格朗日优化即可实现安全策略学习，在基准测试和真实任务中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的顺序决策任务需要在奖励最大化和安全约束之间取得平衡，但这往往导致对抗性优化。现有可达性方法仅处理硬安全约束，很少扩展到累积成本约束。

Method: 首先定义安全条件可达性集合，将奖励最大化与累积安全成本约束解耦；然后利用该集合强制执行安全约束，无需不稳定的min/max或拉格朗日优化；最终实现在固定数据集上离线学习安全策略，无需环境交互。

Result: 在标准离线安全RL基准测试和真实世界海上导航任务中，该方法在保持安全性的同时匹配或超越最先进水平的基线。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的离线安全强化学习算法，通过安全条件可达性集合来解耦奖励最大化和累积安全成本约束，避免了不稳定的min/max或拉格朗日优化。在标准离线安全RL基准测试和真实世界海上导航任务中，该方法在保持安全性的同时达到或超越最先进水平。

Abstract: Sequential decision making using Markov Decision Process underpins many realworld applications. Both model-based and model free methods have achieved strong results in these settings. However, real-world tasks must balance reward maximization with safety constraints, often conflicting objectives, that can lead to unstable min/max, adversarial optimization. A promising alternative is safety reachability analysis, which precomputes a forward-invariant safe state, action set, ensuring that an agent starting inside this set remains safe indefinitely. Yet, most reachability based methods address only hard safety constraints, and little work extends reachability to cumulative cost constraints. To address this, first, we define a safetyconditioned reachability set that decouples reward maximization from cumulative safety cost constraints. Second, we show how this set enforces safety constraints without unstable min/max or Lagrangian optimization, yielding a novel offline safe RL algorithm that learns a safe policy from a fixed dataset without environment interaction. Finally, experiments on standard offline safe RL benchmarks, and a real world maritime navigation task demonstrate that our method matches or outperforms state of the art baselines while maintaining safety.

</details>


### [203] [Behavioral Heterogeneity as Quantum-Inspired Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22729)
*Mohammad Elayan,Wissam Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种量子启发方法，用密度矩阵表示驾驶员的动态潜在状态，通过随机傅里叶特征嵌入行为观测，融合时序持续性与上下文激活机制，在TGSIM等数据集上验证了动态驾驶员profile提取的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前驾驶员异质性研究通常将驾驶员简化为标签或离散模式，这种处理方式将本质上动态连续的行为压缩为静态类别，无法有效捕捉驾驶员行为的时序动态性和上下文敏感性。

Method: 提出了一种量子启发的驾驶员建模方法，将每位驾驶员建模为演化的潜在状态，以密度矩阵形式表示（具备结构化数学属性）。行为观测数据通过非线性随机傅里叶特征（Random Fourier Features）进行嵌入，状态演化融合了行为的时序持续性与上下文相关的profile激活机制。在TGSIM仿真数据和真实驾驶数据上进行了验证。

Result: 该方法能够从驾驶数据中提取并分析驾驶员的行为profile，展示了如何将动态行为表示为演化状态，而非静态分类。

Conclusion: 所提出的量子启发表示方法能够有效地将驾驶员异质性建模为动态演化的潜在状态，通过密度矩阵的数学结构捕捉行为的时空相关性。该方法在TGSIM等数据集上展示了从静态分类向动态行为建模的转变潜力，为理解驾驶员行为模式提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Driver heterogeneity is often reduced to labels or discrete regimes, compressing what is inherently dynamic into static categories. We introduce quantum-inspired representation that models each driver as an evolving latent state, presented as a density matrix with structured mathematical properties. Behavioral observations are embedded via non-linear Random Fourier Features, while state evolution blends temporal persistence of behavior with context-dependent profile activation. We evaluate our approach on empirical driving data, Third Generation Simulation Data (TGSIM), showing how driving profiles are extracted and analyzed.

</details>


### [204] [Efficient Embedding-based Synthetic Data Generation for Complex Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22294)
*Srideepika Jayaraman,Achille Fokoue,Dhaval Patel,Jayant Kalagnanam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM合成数据的质量和多样性，发现嵌入空间密度与预测准确性正相关，提出基于嵌入的采样管道以增强数据多样性，在多个基准测试中取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据生成（SDG）虽能通过微调提升小型LLM性能，但面临数据质量和多样性难以保证的挑战。

Method: 通过分析嵌入空间中生成数据的分布密度，建立密度与预测准确性的关联模型，进而设计针对性的嵌入采样管道。

Result: 实验表明，嵌入空间中特定邻域内示例密度与该区域预测准确性呈强正相关，基于此的采样策略可显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于嵌入的采样管道能够有效增强数据多样性，并在多个基准测试中持续提升模型性能。

Abstract: Synthetic Data Generation (SDG), leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs), has recently been recognized and broadly adopted as an effective approach to improve the performance of smaller but more resource and compute efficient LLMs through fine-tuning. A key challenge in SDG is ensuring the quality and diversity of the generated data. In this paper, we analyze the diversity and distribution of generated data in the embedding space, and demonstrate a strong correlation between the density of examples within a specific neighborhood and the accuracy of predictions on examples drawn from that region. Building on this insight, we present a targeted pipeline for embedding-based sampling that enhances data diversity and consistently improves performance across several benchmarks.

</details>


### [205] [Between the Layers Lies the Truth: Uncertainty Estimation in LLMs Using Intra-Layer Local Information Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22299)
*Zvi N. Badash,Yonatan Belinkov,Moti Freiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于跨层一致性模式的轻量级不确定性估计方法，通过单次前向传播评分内部表征的层间交互，在分布内与探测方法性能相当，跨数据集迁移和量化条件下显著优于探测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型经常自信地犯错误，因此可靠的不确定性估计（UE）至关重要。现有的输出启发式方法廉价但脆弱，而探测内部表征的方法虽然有效但高维且难以迁移。

Method: 提出了一种紧凑的逐实例UE方法，通过单次前向传播对内部表示中的跨层一致性模式进行评分。具体做法是检查不同层之间的层-层交互模式，提取跨层agreement patterns。

Result: 在分布内数据集上，该方法与探测方法性能相当，平均对角线差异仅为-1.8 AUPRC百分点和+4.9 Brier分数点。在跨数据集迁移条件下，一致性优于探测方法，离轴增益高达+2.86 AUPRC和+21.02 Brier点。在4位权重量化下，该方法保持鲁棒性，平均优于探测方法+1.94 AUPRC点和+5.33 Brier分数点。

Conclusion: 提出的跨层一致性方法是一种轻量级、紧凑的LLM不确定性估计方法，能够在单次前向传播中捕获可迁移的不确定性，在跨数据集迁移和量化条件下均表现出优于探测方法的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often confidently wrong, making reliable uncertainty estimation (UE) essential. Output-based heuristics are cheap but brittle, while probing internal representations is effective yet high-dimensional and hard to transfer.
  We propose a compact, per-instance UE method that scores cross-layer agreement patterns in internal representations using a single forward pass.
  Across three models, our method matches probing in-distribution, with mean diagonal differences of at most $-1.8$ AUPRC percentage points and $+4.9$ Brier score points. Under cross-dataset transfer, it consistently outperforms probing, achieving off-diagonal gains up to $+2.86$ AUPRC and $+21.02$ Brier points. Under 4-bit weight-only quantization, it remains robust, improving over probing by $+1.94$ AUPRC points and $+5.33$ Brier points on average.
  Beyond performance, examining specific layer--layer interactions reveals differences in how disparate models encode uncertainty. Altogether, our UE method offers a lightweight, compact means to capture transferable uncertainty in LLMs.

</details>


### [206] [Scaling Attention via Feature Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22300)
*Yan Xie,Tiansheng Wen,Tangda Huang,Bo Chen,Chenyu You,Stefanie Jegelka,Yifei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sparse Feature Attention (SFA)，通过特征级稀疏性而非传统的序列级稀疏性来降低长上下文Transformer的计算成本，实现2.5倍提速和50% FLOPs/KV-cache reduction，同时保持与密集模型相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Transformers扩展到超长上下文受限于自注意力的O(n²d)成本。现有方法沿序列轴通过局部窗口、核近似或token级稀疏性来降低复杂度，但这些方法会导致精度下降。作者转而探索正交轴：特征稀疏性。

Method: 提出Sparse Feature Attention (SFA)，将queries和keys表示为k稀疏码以保持高维表达能力，同时将注意力成本从Θ(n²d)降低到Θ(n²k²/d)。并引入FlashSFA——一个IO感知的内核，扩展FlashAttention以直接在稀疏重叠上操作，无需物化密集评分矩阵。

Result: 在GPT-2和Qwen3预训练中，SFA匹配密集基线，同时提速高达2.5倍，FLOPs和KV-cache减少近50%。在合成和下游基准测试中，SFA保持长上下文的检索准确性和鲁棒性，优于崩溃特征多样性的短嵌入基线。

Conclusion: SFA通过特征稀疏性在长上下文建模中实现了与密集模型相当的性能，同时显著降低了计算成本和内存占用。FlashSFA内核进一步优化了IO效率，使得该方法在实际的超长序列处理中具有实用性。特征级稀疏性作为一个新的优化维度，为Transformer扩展到更长的上下文提供了有价值的思路。

Abstract: Scaling Transformers to ultra-long contexts is bottlenecked by the $O(n^2 d)$ cost of self-attention. Existing methods reduce this cost along the sequence axis through local windows, kernel approximations, or token-level sparsity, but these approaches consistently degrade accuracy. In this paper, we instead explore an orthogonal axis: feature sparsity. We propose Sparse Feature Attention (SFA), where queries and keys are represented as $k$-sparse codes that preserve high-dimensional expressivity while reducing the cost of attention from $Θ(n^2 d)$ to $Θ(n^2 k^2/d)$. To make this efficient at scale, we introduce FlashSFA, an IO-aware kernel that extends FlashAttention to operate directly on sparse overlaps without materializing dense score matrices. Across GPT-2 and Qwen3 pretraining, SFA matches dense baselines while improving speed by up to $2.5\times$ and reducing FLOPs and KV-cache by nearly 50\%. On synthetic and downstream benchmarks, SFA preserves retrieval accuracy and robustness at long contexts, outperforming short-embedding baselines that collapse feature diversity. These results establish feature-level sparsity as a complementary and underexplored axis for efficient attention, enabling Transformers to scale to orders-of-magnitude longer contexts with minimal quality loss. Code is available at https://github.com/YannX1e/Sparse-Feature-Attention.

</details>


### [207] [Latent Semantic Manifolds in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22301)
*Mohamed A. Mabrok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Fisher信息度量的潜在语义流形框架，将LLM离散token输出建模为流形上的Voronoi划分，定义表达性差距衡量词汇离散化带来的语义失真，证明有限词汇量的率-失真下界和线性体积缩放定律，实验验证了6种Transformer架构的普适性规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在连续向量空间中进行内部计算，却输出离散token，这种根本性不匹配的几何后果尚未被深入理解。需要从几何角度量化这种离散化带来的语义失真。

Method: 构建了基于Fisher信息度量的黎曼子流形数学框架，将token对应为Voronoi区域；利用率-失真理论证明有限词汇量的下界；通过coarea公式推导表达性差距的线性体积缩放定律；在6种Transformer架构(124M-1.5B参数)上进行实证验证。

Result: 实验验证了：1)普遍的hourglass型内在维度分布；2)平滑的曲率结构；3)表达性差距与词汇量的线性缩放，斜率0.87-1.12，R²>0.985；4)边界-近端表示的硬核具有规模不变性，提供了perplexity的几何分解。

Conclusion: 研究证明了词汇量大小与语义失真之间存在基本权衡，并揭示了LLM表征中存在一个与模型规模无关的边界-近端表示硬核。这为架构设计、模型压缩、解码策略和缩放定律提供了几何理论基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform internal computations in continuous vector spaces yet produce discrete tokens -- a fundamental mismatch whose geometric consequences remain poorly understood. We develop a mathematical framework that interprets LLM hidden states as points on a latent semantic manifold: a Riemannian submanifold equipped with the Fisher information metric, where tokens correspond to Voronoi regions partitioning the manifold. We define the expressibility gap, a geometric measure of the semantic distortion from vocabulary discretization, and prove two theorems: a rate-distortion lower bound on distortion for any finite vocabulary, and a linear volume scaling law for the expressibility gap via the coarea formula. We validate these predictions across six transformer architectures (124M-1.5B parameters), confirming universal hourglass intrinsic dimension profiles, smooth curvature structure, and linear gap scaling with slopes 0.87-1.12 (R^2 > 0.985). The margin distribution across models reveals a persistent hard core of boundary-proximal representations invariant to scale, providing a geometric decomposition of perplexity. We discuss implications for architecture design, model compression, decoding strategies, and scaling laws

</details>


### [208] [A Theoretical Framework for Energy-Aware Gradient Pruning in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22465)
*Emmanouil M. Athanasakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中边缘设备的能量约束问题，提出Cost-Weighted Magnitude Pruning (CWMP)方法，将参数剪枝形式化为考虑硬件级差异的能量约束投影问题，在非IID CIFAR-10上实现更优的性能-能量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习受限于边缘设备的通信和能源约束。现有梯度稀疏化方法（如Top-K剪枝）虽然有效降低通信负载，但未考虑能源效率，假设所有参数更新具有相同的传输和内存更新成本，忽略了硬件现实差异。

Method: 将剪枝形式化为能量约束投影问题，提出基于成本加权幅值的剪枝规则CWMP，优先考虑参数更新的幅值与其物理成本的相对关系。提供了CWMP作为最优贪心解的证明及其全局能量效率的概率分析。

Result: 在非IID CIFAR-10基准测试中，CWMP始终建立更优的性能-能量Pareto前沿，相比Top-K基线方法表现更好。

Conclusion: CWMP作为能量约束的剪枝方法，在非IID CIFAR-10基准测试中建立了卓越的性能-能量Pareto前沿，优于Top-K基线方法。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is constrained by the communication and energy limitations of decentralized edge devices. While gradient sparsification via Top-K magnitude pruning effectively reduces the communication payload, it remains inherently energy-agnostic. It assumes all parameter updates incur identical downstream transmission and memory-update costs, ignoring hardware realities. We formalize the pruning process as an energy-constrained projection problem that accounts for the hardware-level disparities between memory-intensive and compute-efficient operations during the post-backpropagation phase. We propose Cost-Weighted Magnitude Pruning (CWMP), a selection rule that prioritizes parameter updates based on their magnitude relative to their physical cost. We demonstrate that CWMP is the optimal greedy solution to this constrained projection and provide a probabilistic analysis of its global energy efficiency. Numerical results on a non-IID CIFAR-10 benchmark show that CWMP consistently establishes a superior performance-energy Pareto frontier compared to the Top-K baseline.

</details>


### [209] [Research on Individual Trait Clustering and Development Pathway Adaptation Based on the K-means Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22302)
*Qianru Wei,Jihaoyu Yang,Cheng Zhang,Jinming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用K-means聚类算法对3000多名大学生的CET-4成绩、GPA、人格特质和学生干部经历进行分析，将学生分为四类群体，基于聚类结果为每类学生提供针对性职业指导建议，证明了不同特征组合的学生适合不同职业方向，为个性化职业指导提供科学依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着信息技术发展，AI和机器学习在教育领域展现潜力。现有方法多聚焦于职业路径预测，较少研究不同特征组合学生在特定职业方向的适配性，因此需要探索如何利用K-means聚类算法为大学生提供精准职业指导。

Method: 使用K-means聚类算法对学生数据进行分类，通过最小化簇内平方误差将相似特征的学生分到同一组，确保组内学生特征高度相似，组间差异最大化。将3000多名学生按CET-4成绩、GPA、人格特质和学生干部经历分为四个主要群体。

Result: 具有不同特征组合的学生适合不同的职业方向，研究结果为个性化职业指导提供了科学依据，有效提升了学生就业成功率。

Conclusion: 研究证明了K-means聚类算法在大学生职业指导中的有效性，不同特征组合的学生适合不同职业方向，为个性化职业指导提供了科学依据，有效提升了学生就业成功率。未来可通过扩大样本量、增加特征变量和考虑外部因素来提高聚类精度和指导效果。

Abstract: With the development of information technology, the application of artificial intelligence and machine learning in the field of education shows great potential. This study aims to explore how to utilize K-means clustering algorithm to provide accurate career guidance for college students. Existing methods mostly focus on the prediction of career paths, but there are fewer studies on the fitness of students with different combinations of characteristics in specific career directions. In this study, we analyze the data of more than 3000 students on their CET-4 scores, GPA, personality traits and student cadre experiences, and use the K-means clustering algorithm to classify the students into four main groups. The K-means clustering algorithm groups students with similar characteristics into one group by minimizing the intra-cluster squared error, ensuring that the students within the same cluster are highly similar in their characteristics, and that differences between different clusters are maximized. Based on the clustering results, targeted career guidance suggestions are provided for each group. The results of the study show that students with different combinations of characteristics are suitable for different career directions, which provides a scientific basis for personalized career guidance and effectively enhances students' employment success rate. Future research can further improve the precision of clustering and the guidance effect by expanding the sample size, increasing the feature variables and considering external factors.

</details>


### [210] [Sample Transform Cost-Based Training-Free Hallucination Detector for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22303)
*Zeyang Ding,Xinglin Hu,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用最优传输距离计算Wasserstein距离矩阵来量化LLM条件分布的复杂度，从而检测幻觉。该方法无需训练即可工作，通过AvgWD和EigenWD两个互补信号实现轻量级幻觉检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型中的幻觉问题仍是可信部署的主要障碍，需要准确、轻量且广泛适用的检测器。作者认为LLM给定提示词后的条件分布复杂度可作为幻觉的指标。

Method: 提出基于最优传输的Wasserstein距离矩阵方法，计算成对样本token嵌入之间的最优传输距离，推导两个互补信号：AvgWD（平均成本）和EigenWD（成本复杂度），无需训练即可检测幻觉。并扩展到黑盒LLM场景。

Result: 实验表明AvgWD和EigenWD与强不确定性基线相比具有竞争力，在不同模型和数据集上提供互补行为，验证了分布复杂度作为LLM真实性信号的有效性。

Conclusion: 分布复杂度是LLM真实性的有效信号，AvgWD和EigenWD在不同模型和数据集上提供互补行为，验证了基于Wasserstein距离的幻觉检测方法的有效性。

Abstract: Hallucinations in large language models (LLMs) remain a central obstacle to trustworthy deployment, motivating detectors that are accurate, lightweight, and broadly applicable. Since an LLM with a prompt defines a conditional distribution, we argue that the complexity of the distribution is an indicator of hallucination. However, the density of the distribution is unknown and the samples (i.e., responses generated for the prompt) are discrete distributions, which leads to a significant challenge in quantifying the complexity of the distribution. We propose to compute the optimal-transport distances between the sets of token embeddings of pairwise samples, which yields a Wasserstein distance matrix measuring the costs of transforming between the samples. This Wasserstein distance matrix provides a means to quantify the complexity of the distribution defined by the LLM with the prompt. Based on the Wasserstein distance matrix, we derive two complementary signals: AvgWD, measuring the average cost, and EigenWD, measuring the cost complexity. This leads to a training-free detector for hallucinations in LLMs. We further extend the framework to black-box LLMs via teacher forcing with an accessible teacher model. Experiments show that AvgWD and EigenWD are competitive with strong uncertainty baselines and provide complementary behavior across models and datasets, highlighting distribution complexity as an effective signal for LLM truthfulness.

</details>


### [211] [Mitigating Premature Discretization with Progressive Quantization for Robust Vector Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22304)
*Wenhao Zhao,Qiran Zou,Zhouhan Lin,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProVQ通过渐进式量化策略解决VQ中的过早离散化问题，将量化视为从连续到离散空间的课程学习过程，在图像生成和蛋白质结构标记化任务上均取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VQ范式存在一个根本性冲突：它们在编码器尚未捕获底层数据流形之前就强制执行离散化，这种现象被称为"过早离散化"（Premature Discretization），是当前方法的核心缺陷。

Method: ProVQ将量化硬度作为训练过程中的动态轴，采用课程学习的方式，逐步将潜在空间从连续状态Anneals至离散状态。这种渐进式方法能够有效引导码本向充分展开的流形区域移动。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和ImageNet-100基准上实现了改进的重建和生成性能；在StrutTokenBench排行榜上建立了蛋白质结构标记化的新性能上限。

Conclusion: ProVQ通过将量化过程视为从连续潜在空间到离散空间的课程，成功解决了现有VQ方法中的过早离散化问题。该方法在图像生成和蛋白质结构标记化两个不同领域都展现出显著的性能提升，证明了其广泛的适用性和有效性。

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) has become the cornerstone of tokenization for many multimodal Large Language Models and diffusion synthesis. However, existing VQ paradigms suffer from a fundamental conflict: they enforce discretization before the encoder has captured the underlying data manifold.
